- **no-ai-signoffs**: Drop the assistant-style greetings, sign-offs, and hedging that mark text as AI-generated. Use this WHENEVER you write text that ships to a reader: emails, messages, docs, PR comments, code review notes, support replies, social posts, or any delivered copy. Openers like "Certainly!", "Great question!", "Sure, I can help with that", and closers like "I hope this helps!", "Let me know if you have any questions!", "Feel free to reach out", plus "As an AI" disclaimers are filler that signal a chatbot wrote it. Open at the actual content and end when the content ends. ([TheArmagan/skills](https://github.com/TheArmagan/skills/tree/HEAD/no-ai-signoffs) · ★3)

- **no-getbets**: Hard ban on any reference to the external domain getbets.us on cruisinginthewake.com. Fires automatically on every Write/Edit/MultiEdit and every commit; no manual invocation. - **no-ghost-edit-mode**: Production CLAUDE.md for Claude Code. Enforces phased execution, edit integrity, exhaustive reference search, and scope discipline on agentic coding tasks. Drop into project root as CLAUDE.md. Trigger when configuring Claude Code agent behaviour, or when agent is making silent bad edits, skipping verification, or breaking things without warning. ([sarankirthic/my-claude-skills](https://github.com/sarankirthic/my-claude-skills/tree/HEAD/no-ghost-edit-mode))
- **no-ghosts**: Apply whenever writing or editing any prose artifact or test - docs, code comments, PR descriptions, commit messages, issues, READMEs. Keeps edit history out of the text. ([sounkou-bioinfo/pi-skills](https://github.com/sounkou-bioinfo/pi-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/no-ghosts) · ★2)
- **no-git-backup**: git을 몰라도 작업물을 안전하게 지키는 백업 습관을 잡아 줍니다. "백업 어떻게 해요" 같은 요청에 사용. ([x77xdavid-prog/checkup-skills](https://github.com/x77xdavid-prog/checkup-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/no-git-backup/skills/no-git-backup))
- **no-git-signature**: Prevents Claude Code signature and attribution lines from appearing in git commits, PR descriptions, branch names, or any git-related text. - **no-github-backlog**: Drain a GitHub issue backlog autonomously. Spawns isolated subagents per issue across 7 stages (investigate, implement, review, security-review, qa, fix, merge), logs every stage to backlog.csv. Use when user asks to clear, drain, close, or resolve an issue backlog. ([dzianisv/skills](https://github.com/dzianisv/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/no-github-backlog) · ★4)
- **no-github-commit-without-ask**: Use this whenever any bot might commit or push to GitHub — standing rule: never without an explicit ask. ([Shixuuu/grokbot-pi-templates](https://github.com/Shixuuu/grokbot-pi-templates/tree/HEAD/templates/community/teams-handoffs/bot-father/skills/no-github-commit-without-ask) · ★6)
- **no-glaze**: Always-on engineering posture. Strips sycophancy and adds the behaviors it hides: refusing to confabulate, surfacing contradictions when tool results disagree with claims, refusing to capitulate under social pressure without new substance, naming bad ideas as bad, policing scope in both directions.
